# S1.1c-7 whole-code survey — every finding

Grouped for the plan check; within a group, by package then severity. `Src` is the survey batch and its row (b = first pass, p = second pass over files the first pass read only in part). Each finding is an agent's claim, checked by GitNexus or grep as its row says; the coordinator verified a sample (see the summary). Ids are stable: `<group>-<n>`.


## G1 — Blocker candidates (gate checks that miss what they claim) (3)

| Id | Src | Location | Element | Kind | Exported | Finding | Proposed fix | Why | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1-1 | b11b#1 | gate/runner/rows/lawdelta.py:186 (docstring :13-14) | `_block` → `git.numstat`; also `git.ls_files`, `git.head`, `git.dirty` in `main` :220/:236/:250 | code | n/a | The docstring says "A git command outside its contract is a failure (DA-S3)". But `git.numstat`, `ls_files`, `head` and `dirty` (gate/runner/git.py:40-62, :99) throw away git's exit code. So a failed `git diff --numstat` returns [] and the row prints an empty LAW Δ and passes. A failed `ls_files` gives "git tracks nothing there" for every path. | Make numstat, ls_files, head and dirty raise `git.GitError` when the exit code is not 0, as `grep_files` and `ls_tree` already do. `main` already catches GitError around `_block` and `exemptions`; extend that to the other calls. | GitNexus: `_block` calls numstat and read_blobs. Only lawdelta and test_git call `ls_files`. The `except git.GitError` at :242 can never fire for numstat. Gate layer, LAW Δ review aid. | should-fix |
| G1-2 | b10#1 | tools/archcheck/main.go:712 (used at :789) | `(*checker).written` | method | no | The globals check treats a write as `=`, `++`/`--` or `delete(x, …)` only. `clear(x)` is not counted, so a package-level map cleared with `clear(table)` passes as a "never-written lookup table". The Cases-side twin `writes` (:1550) does count `clear`. | Count `clear` in `written`. Better: make `written` call `writes` so the two cannot drift. Add a test with a cleared table that must be red. | GitNexus: `written` is called only by `globals` (flow "Globals → Root"), the sole gate for ADR-0015 §2.2's globals rule. `writes` is called by `binding` (flow "Walk → Root"). Both use `root`. | blocker |
| G1-3 | b10#2 | tools/archcheck/main.go:788 | `globals`, Must case | code | n/a | The package doc (:22-24) allows a Must-constructed var "built from constants, never written". The code accepts any `x.Must*` call with neither check, so `var re = regexp.MustCompile(s)` later reassigned (`re = …`) passes. Error sentinels (:787) can also be reassigned freely. No test covers a written Must var (grep: only :227, :230). | Add `&& !written[id.Name]` to the Must and sentinel cases. Otherwise correct the doc. Add a red test. | GitNexus: `globals` ← `CheckSources`, and its callees are `call`, `mustConstructor`, `table` and `written`. The never-written proof is there but wired only to the table case. | blocker |

## G10 — Behaviour defects in production code (23)

| Id | Src | Location | Element | Kind | Exported | Finding | Proposed fix | Why | Severity |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| G10-1 | b09#13 | apphandle.go:43-64 | `AppHandle.Start` | method | yes | Start calls `Activate` first, then writes the tables, then runs the sources. If a `TableWrite` fails (for example the context ends partway), the app stays active with some table rows and no sources. A retry gets ErrStarted, so the sources can never be opened. Stop, Undeploy and a new Deploy is the only way back. | Write the starting rows as part of activation, or on failure undo the activation (Drain), and document the rule. | GitNexus: callers are `fcStart`, `Example_embed`, `TestLifecycleTable`, `imApp` (io/inmemory) and others. ErrStarted on a second Start is pinned by lifecycle_test.go:49. | should-fix |
| G10-2 | b16#1 | gate/bench/escapes.py:340 | `check` (`old = _texts(before, {original_before})`) | code | n/a | The two sides count different groups. Before counts heap lines for the original function only; after counts the original plus every non-inlined `--helper`. A helper that already existed before, was not inlined, and has heap lines shows up as "new" and gives a false exit 1 "costs". The leak side already handles this case (`members_before` includes helpers in `before.functions`, line 347). | Count the before side over `{original_before, *(h for h in helpers if h in before.functions and h not in before.inlined)}` and add a test where a pre-existing helper has a heap line. | GitNexus: `check` is called only by `main` and calls `_texts`, `build` and `resolve`. `test_the_before_side_lists_the_helpers_that_existed_before` passes a pre-existing helper (`store`), so the tool expects that case, but `store` has no heap line and the gap is never hit. Hand-run measuring tool behind the split cost verdicts. | should-fix |
| G10-3 | b16#11 | gate/bench/load.py:95; gate/bench/mutate.py:211 | `_Parser(prog=…)` | code | n/a | Both keep argparse's default `add_help`, so `--help` prints usage and raises `SystemExit(0)`. `main`'s `except BaseException` catches it, prints "error: internal error: SystemExit: 0" and exits 2. compare.py and escapes.py pass `add_help=False`. | Pass `add_help=False`, as compare and escapes do, or let `SystemExit` through `main`. | From reading `main` at load.py:142-153 and mutate.py:289-300. | should-fix |
| G10-4 | b16#20 | gate/bench/compare.py:116 | duplicate-benchmark error | code | n/a | Says "reported a benchmark twice" but lists every name, not the repeated one. | List `[n for n, k in Counter(names).items() if k > 1]`. | `_run` is called by `_time_pass`/`_alloc_pass` (GitNexus `_run` → `parse_bench`). | later |
| G10-5 | b14#1 | gate/hooks/gitnexus_reindex.py:73-81, 122-128 | `running` / `start` (pidfile guard) | func | no | The guard checks and then starts, with no lock. Two hook calls at once (a prompt plus a Bash PostToolUse, or two Bash calls) can both see "not running" and both start `gitnexus analyze --force` on the same storage. Separately, `os.kill(pid, 0)` raising EPERM (the pid is alive but owned by someone else) counts as "not running", and a reused pid counts as "running". The docstring claims "A pidfile ... keeps a second run from starting". \| Take an exclusive `O_CREAT\ | O_EXCL` (or `fcntl.flock`) lock on the pidfile before starting the follow-up, and treat `PermissionError` as alive. | GitNexus: `running` has one caller, `main`. The hook runs on every prompt and every git Bash call, so overlapping calls are its normal case. | should-fix |
| G10-6 | b11#9 | gate/runner/git.py:40-44,51-62,99-113,32-33 | `ls_files`, `numstat`, `dirty`, `head` | func | no | These ignore git's exit code. A failing git reads as no files, no changes, 0 edits or "". | Raise `GitError` on a non-zero exit, as `grep_files` and `ls_tree` already do. | GitNexus: `dirty` is called by `rows/lawdelta.main`, so a broken `git status` reads as a clean tree. | should-fix |
| G10-7 | b11b#3 | gate/runner/rows/suppressions.py:159 | `ruff_pin` (`waived` comprehension) | code | n/a | `lint.per-file-ignores` is taken out of the whole-file pin at :152, so its shape is never checked. A list instead of a table raises AttributeError on `.items()` and the row crashes. A string value such as `"E501"` is iterated one character at a time, giving faults for codes `E`, `5`, `0`, `1`. | Check that the value is a table of string lists and return `ruff.toml: lint.per-file-ignores is not a table of code lists` otherwise. | GitNexus: `main` calls `ruff_pin`, and so do tests in test_rows_ruff, test_suppressions and test_breakit_s11c2. `main` has no try around the call. | should-fix |
| G10-8 | b11b#2 | gate/runner/rows/vulns.py:99-103, :75-80 | `open_copy`, `_json_file` | code | n/a | Both catch only `FileNotFoundError`. A vulndb.zip, stamp.json or fetch-error.txt that is a directory or unreadable raises PermissionError or IsADirectoryError out of `scan`. `main` does not catch it, so the row dies with a traceback and no named fault. Every other fault in this module is a named line. | Catch `OSError` and name it: `vulns: cannot read {path}: {e.strerror}: run make vulndb`. | GitNexus: `scan` is the only caller of `open_copy`, and `open_copy` calls `_json_file`, `vulndb._time` and `check_db`. No wrapper sits above `scan` in `main`. | should-fix |
